### 1. Annotated Coffee Table Mood Board

<img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/EvoLinkAI/awesome-gpt-image-2-API-and-Prompts/main/images/ui_case130/output.jpg" alt="Annotated Coffee Table Mood Board" width="480">

- **Categorie:** UI & Social Media
- **Fonte:** [EvoLinkAI/awesome-gpt-image-2-prompts](https://x.com/Ciri_ai/status/2049721047696732550)

**Prompt:**

```text
Use the uploaded photo as the main base image.

Based on the coffee, drinks, desserts, plates, props, table setting, and overall composition shown in the image, create an aesthetic and emotional result that naturally reflects the real elements in the photo. Do not add any people. Stay true to the actual types of food and drinks, their arrangement, and the atmosphere captured in the original image.

Then, carefully observe each element in the photo and add hand-drawn style annotations that match each subject:

Use white pen-style sketch lines with a rough, slightly uneven, hand-drawn feel

Include object outlines, arrows, and dotted lines to guide visual flow

Add short handwritten Korean notes in a casual, introspective, emotional tone

For drinks: describe taste, temperature, and mood

For desserts: describe texture and flavor

For the space: describe the ambiance

Include a short overall summary of the scene

Subtly incorporate small details like steam, sparkles, hearts, or tiny emotive symbols

Maintain a clean, spacious layout with an Instagram story or magazine memo-like aesthetic

The final result should look like an emotionally styled version of the original photo, without forcing in any elements that don’t naturally belong to it
```

<a id="prompt-03e49c90e7c7d18e8c89"></a>

### 2. Post sui social media - Ritratti di coppia in stile Fujifilm

<img src="https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1777971024975_lbj1i7_HHfBSpYXUAARWKh.jpg" alt="Post sui social media - Ritratti di coppia in stile Fujifilm" width="480">

A detailed prompt for generating a 3x3 grid of Japanese home-style couple photos with a Fujifilm film aesthetic.

- **Categorie:** Photography & Portrait
- **Fonte:** [YouMind-OpenLab/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://x.com/zhongying14/status/2051322234082410749)

**Prompt:**

```text
Basandosi sulle foto reali di due giovani adulti caricate dall'utente, genera un "collage di album a griglia 3x3 {argument name="photo style" default="ritratto di coppia in stile domestico giapponese Fujifilm"}".

Preserva rigorosamente le caratteristiche identitarie reali delle due persone. Sia la ragazza che il ragazzo devono assomigliare chiaramente alle persone originali nelle foto caricate, inclusi forma del viso, proporzioni facciali, struttura di occhi/sopracciglia, naso, labbra, linea della mascella, tono della pelle, età, attaccatura dei capelli, caratteristiche dell'acconciatura e temperamento generale. Non trasformarli in volti occidentali, stili da studio coreano, influencer o estranei eccessivamente ritoccati. Tutte le 9 piccole foto finali devono ritrarre la stessa coppia reale, catturata da diverse angolazioni, distanze e momenti durante la stessa sessione domestica.

## Formato Visivo
Genera un collage 3x3 con 9 piccole foto, rapporto complessivo 1:1. Utilizza linee di separazione sottili {argument name="border color" default="nere"} tra le foto, il tutto posizionato su uno sfondo {argument name="background color" default="nero"}, come un album fotografico domestico curato. Non un poster commerciale o un layout da studio; evita modelli ripetitivi.

## Stile Visivo Principale
Stile generale: fotografia di lifestyle giapponese, texture della fotocamera Fujifilm, ritratto di coppia reale in casa, scatti spontanei, delicati, rilassati, tranquilli e narrativi.
Concentrati sui colori e sull'atmosfera delle pellicole Fujifilm come Superia, Pro 400H o C200. Le immagini dovrebbero avere una bassa saturazione senza apparire grigie, luci naturali morbide, toni della pelle trasparenti ma sobri, leggera grana della pellicola, leggero rumore nelle ombre e contrasto morbido. Dovrebbe sembrare una foto lifestyle Fujifilm ariosa e stratificata.
Evita: texture da vecchio iPhone, compressione mobile, nitidezza digitale, filtri retrò pesanti, pubblicità commerciali o stili e-commerce.

## Relazione tra i Personaggi
I due sono una {argument name="relationship" default="giovane coppia reale, intima e naturale"}. Ogni fotogramma deve mostrare chiaramente che sono una coppia con un senso di familiarità e compagnia. Le interazioni devono essere realistiche e rilassate: avvicinarsi, stabilire un contatto visivo, guardare in basso e ridere, appoggiarsi su una spalla, leggere insieme, passarsi oggetti, preparare cibo, sistemarsi i vestiti, guardarsi allo specchio o osservarsi da una porta. Intimi ma sobri: niente eccessi, niente erotismo e nessuna recitazione in posa da matrimonio.

## Espressioni e Angolazioni
Le 9 foto non devono guardare tutte la fotocamera o sorridere solo leggermente. Le espressioni devono essere ricche e varie: ridacchiare, essere presi in giro, guardare in basso ridendo, tranquilli, sognanti, sguardi gentili, chiacchiere rilassate, concentrati su un compito, rispondere con uno sguardo indietro, chiudere gli occhi ridendo, guardare fuori dalla finestra o guardarsi l'un l'altro.
Le angolazioni del viso devono variare significativamente. Non ripetere lo stesso schema. Usa un mix di: frontale, semi-frontale, profilo, sguardo verso il basso, sguardo all'indietro, sguardo leggermente verso l'alto, quasi di schiena, angolazioni allo specchio, osservazione a distanza e dettagli di vita in primo piano. Ogni fotogramma deve avere una nuova direzione della testa e un ritmo visivo diverso.

## Requisiti di Abbigliamento
Non utilizzare gli abiti originali delle foto caricate. Cambiali in outfit naturali da lifestyle giapponese: bassa saturazione, colori neutri, materiali morbidi. Colori come bianco crema, farina d'avena, grigio chiaro, caffè pallido, blu nebbia o antracite. Gli articoli includono cardigan in maglia, t-shirt morbide, camicie di lino, abbigliamento da casa, gonne lunghe o calzini. Dovrebbero sembrare vestiti comodi che una coppia reale indossa a casa.

## Ambientazione Domestica
Un appartamento pulito e ordinato con tracce di vita reale (non una casa modello). Includi elementi come tende, pavimenti in legno, divani, cuscini, coperte, libri, album, tazze, piatti, piante, lampade da letto, specchi o tavoli: tutti come sottili aiuti per concentrarsi sulla coppia.

## Contenuto dei 9 Fotogrammi
1. Tranquillità alla finestra: Il ragazzo guarda fuori; la ragazza si appoggia naturalmente. Atmosfera tranquilla.
2. Lettura sul divano: Leggono un libro insieme; uno si appoggia sulla spalla dell'altro.
3. Passare un piatto: Al tavolo da pranzo, passarsi la frutta; movimento attivo e contatto visivo.
4. Preparazione in cucina: Preparano il cibo; uno taglia la frutta mentre l'altro osserva e ride.
5. Vista dalla porta: Prospettiva dell'osservatore attraverso l'intelaiatura di una porta verso il soggiorno.
6. Tenerezza in camera da letto: Seduti sul letto; la ragazza sistema delicatamente il colletto del ragazzo.
7. Foto sul pavimento: Seduti sul tappeto a guardare riviste insieme; pose sedute spontanee.
8. Frammento allo specchio: Catturato attraverso uno specchio; sistemarsi i capelli o un breve sguardo allo specchio.
9. Pausa sul balcone: In piedi vicino alla porta scorrevole; uno guarda fuori mentre l'altro si avvicina con un sorriso.

## Consistenza e Stabilità
Mantieni la coerenza facciale in tutti i 9 fotogrammi. Evita gesti complessi con le mani (niente mani sul viso o dita incrociate). La qualità dovrebbe includere una leggera grana della pellicola e una texture di scansione per sembrare un set reale, non una griglia AI perfetta. Evita artefatti digitali, arti extra o testi strani.
```

### 3. Post sui social media - Diario con adesivi chibi clone

<img src="https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1777971003063_000rup_HHf9C1UWMAAOPD4.jpg" alt="Post sui social media - Diario con adesivi chibi clone" width="480">

A creative prompt that transforms a real photo into a scrapbook diary style with multiple chibi clones of the subject.

- **Categorie:** UI & Social Media
- **Fonte:** [YouMind-OpenLab/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://x.com/miilesus/status/2051386554015449281)

**Prompt:**

```text
Crea un'immagine di alta qualità “{argument name="style" default="chibi clone sticker diary photo"}” basata sulla foto reale caricata. Preserva l'identità, il volto, l'acconciatura, il colore dei capelli, l'abbigliamento, le proporzioni corporee, la posa, l'illuminazione e lo sfondo della persona originale. Non alterare i tratti del viso né trasformare il soggetto in un'illustrazione completa: mantieni un aspetto fotografico realistico.
Analizza la scena come un momento di vita quotidiana dedicato a {argument name="activity" default="fitness/running"}. Aggiungi 5-8 mini cloni chibi della stessa persona attorno al soggetto, progettati in un coerente stile adesivo kawaii (testa grande, corpo piccolo, grandi occhi espressivi, finitura digitale pulita). Ogni clone deve assomigliare chiaramente alla persona reale (stessi capelli, abbigliamento, colori).
Progetta ogni chibi con {argument name="action theme" default="running-related actions"} ed emozioni differenti: fare jogging, fare stretching, bere acqua, sentirsi stanchi, fare il tifo, fare il pollice in su, festeggiare il traguardo. Assicurati che tutte le pose siano uniche e contestualmente pertinenti.
Renderizza ogni chibi come un adesivo con bordi bianchi, ombre morbide e un leggero effetto fluttuante. Disponili attorno al soggetto e lungo i bordi senza coprire il viso o il corpo principale.
Aggiungi leggeri scarabocchi disegnati a mano (cuori, scintille, frecce, linee di movimento, cerchi) in bianco con sottili accenti rosa, mantenendo un aspetto pulito da diario scrapbook.
Includi 5-8 brevi frasi in stile calligrafico che richiamino un mood fitness (carino, energico, incoraggiante). Usa principalmente testo bianco con lievi riflessi rosa e piccoli segni decorativi.
Composizione: mantieni la persona reale come focus centrale, circondata da adesivi chibi e scarabocchi. Il risultato dovrebbe sembrare un'immagine di diario lifestyle per i social media, curata, giocosa e ad alta risoluzione: pulita, equilibrata e visivamente ricca senza risultare confusa.
```

### 5. Post sui social media - Foto di strada di Akihabara anni '90

<img src="https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1777971070679_ltevk4_HHeu6VDbAAAijQh.jpg" alt="Post sui social media - Foto di strada di Akihabara anni '90" width="480">

Generates a realistic VHS-style 1990s Akihabara electronics district street scene for retro documentary or historical urban imagery.

- **Categorie:** UI & Social Media
- **Fonte:** [YouMind-OpenLab/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://x.com/pipipimayomayo/status/2051300477275660460#reversed-0)

**Prompt:**

```text
Crea una fotografia di strada documentaristica fotorealistica di {argument name="location and era" default="Akihabara, Tokyo negli anni '90"}, scattata ad altezza occhi in una stretta via dello shopping di elettronica al crepuscolo o nel tardo pomeriggio. La scena contiene circa 30 pedoni visibili, per lo più uomini adulti con abbigliamento casual anni '90 come giacche di jeans, maglioni a quadri, jeans chiari, giubbotti bomber, abiti formali, borse a tracolla e buste della spesa; i volti sono morbidi, indistinti o sfocati dal movimento, come in un vecchio fermo immagine televisivo. La strada è affollata ma non caotica, con persone in piedi, intente a guardare le vetrine, che si allontanano dalla fotocamera o si radunano vicino ai negozi. Entrambi i lati della strada sono fiancheggiati da alti edifici commerciali coperti da una fitta segnaletica di negozi di elettronica giapponesi, inclusi grandi cartelli verticali e orizzontali con frammenti leggibili come {argument name="main sign text" default="オノデン"}, {argument name="store brand text" default="Panasonic"}, “FAN”, “BEST” e altre insegne di negozi in katakana. Il lato sinistro presenta una prominente tettoia blu e gialla di un negozio di elettronica, mentre il lato destro ha una tenda da sole arancione e blu, scaffali, poster e merce in scatola esposta all'ingresso. Cavi elettrici aerei si incrociano nel cielo luminoso e sbiadito tra gli edifici di media altezza. Utilizza un'estetica realistica da videocamera consumer giapponese degli anni '90 o da trasmissione VHS: formato 4:3, risoluzione leggermente bassa, morbidezza interlacciata, colori tenui, leggera grana della pellicola, alte luci bruciate nel cielo, sottile distorsione a barilotto, naturale disordine della folla e assenza di smartphone moderni, cartelloni LED, moda contemporanea o elementi futuristici.
```

### 8. Post sui social media - Overlay adesivo Chibi da palestra

È una giovane nobile dall'aspetto delicato, che indossa un Hanfu azzurro pallido semitrasparente con maniche di garza a strati, sottili ricami floreali lungo il colletto e una morbida veste interna bianca. ### 10. Profilo / Avatar - Trasformazione in nuvola atmosferica

<img src="https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1777971006989_nw6o12_HHgfIlAaAAAt078.jpg" alt="Profilo / Avatar - Trasformazione in nuvola atmosferica" width="480">

An ethereal image prompt that transforms a subject's face into a soft, atmospheric cloud composition within a clear blue sky.

- **Categorie:** UI & Social Media
- **Fonte:** [YouMind-OpenLab/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://x.com/xRahultripathi/status/2051423866355085679)

**Prompt:**

```text
Trasforma {argument name="subject" default="[scrivi qui]"} in una composizione di nuvole soffici e atmosferiche, preservando i tratti del volto, l'espressione e l'identità riconoscibili del soggetto.
Il volto dovrebbe apparire sottilmente formato dalle nuvole, fondendosi naturalmente in uno sfondo di cielo azzurro brillante. Utilizza un'illuminazione soffusa e diffusa con riflessi delicati e ombre ariose per creare profondità e realismo.
Assicurati che la trasformazione sia simbolica piuttosto che letterale: evita bordi netti, texture della pelle o dettagli nitidi. Il soggetto dovrebbe dare l'impressione di emergere o dissolversi tra le nuvole.
Mantieni le proporzioni del viso, gli occhi, il sorriso e i tratti distintivi chiaramente visibili attraverso la struttura nuvolosa.
Stile: onirico, etereo, surreale, cinematografico
Illuminazione: luce solare naturale, bagliore soffuso, luce volumetrica
Palette di colori: azzurro cielo, bianco, sfumature tenui
Atmosfera: pacifica, edificante, ispiratrice
Aggiungi sottili strati di nuvole attorno e all'interno del volto per una fusione senza interruzioni. Lo sfondo dovrebbe essere un cielo azzurro pulito con tenui sfumature di nuvole.
Alta risoluzione, texture nuvolosa ultra-realistica, fusione fluida, senza testo, senza filigrana.
```

Generates a cinematic hyper-realistic bus selfie scene where a city street outside the window transforms into a dreamy kite-filled sunset.

- **Fonte:** [YouMind-OpenLab/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://x.com/AiwithLariab/status/2051334366908059812#reversed-0)

**Prompt:**

```text
Crea un'immagine cinematografica iperrealistica dall'interno di un autobus urbano in movimento, scattata come un selfie grandangolare da un passeggero seduto accanto a un grande finestrino. In primo piano a sinistra, mostra {argument name="subject description" default="una giovane donna con capelli castani mossi, che indossa un blazer nero e una piccola collana"} mentre allunga un braccio verso la fotocamera in una posa da selfie casuale, con un'espressione calma e un sorriso accennato, parzialmente retroilluminata dalla calda luce diurna. L'interno dell'autobus è visibile dietro di lei con sedili a motivi, mancorrenti gialli, passeggeri sfocati e una profondità di campo ridotta. Il grande finestrino domina il lato destro e funge sia da vetro che da portale: riflette il soggetto e l'interno dell'autobus rivelando al contempo una strada cittadina surreale all'esterno. Oltre il vetro, mostra un viale bagnato e riflettente fiancheggiato da eleganti edifici antichi, pedoni che si muovono al rallentatore, una persona che tiene un ombrello rosso e un'auto con i fari accesi. La strada ordinaria si trasforma gradualmente in un paesaggio onirico con esattamente 9 aquiloni colorati che fluttuano in un cielo al tramonto rosa e blu, particelle luminose sospese nell'aria, colori cinematografici caldi ed esagerati e riflessi sia dell'interno reale dell'autobus che del mondo surreale esterno sovrapposti sul finestrino. Usa un'illuminazione {argument name="time of day" default="tramonto durante la golden hour"} con luce diurna naturale sul viso che si fonde in un bagliore da sogno con riflessi caldi, grana cinematografica morbida, riflessi dell'obiettivo realistici, sottile sfocatura da movimento e un'atmosfera pacifica di immaginazione, evasione e quieta felicità.
```

<a id="prompt-93c4f6b7663b208ef32d"></a>

### 22. 前后端数据库 SaaS 信息图

<img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/EvoLinkAI/awesome-gpt-image-2-prompts/main/images/ui_case122/output.jpg" alt="前后端数据库 SaaS 信息图" width="480">

- **Categorie:** UI & Social Media
- **Fonte:** [EvoLinkAI/awesome-gpt-image-2-prompts](https://x.com/alantech_/status/2050462324486783060)

**Prompt:**

```text
gpt image 2生图能力真的恐怖啊，这是我的提示词： 01 前端（用户看到的部分） 是什么：前端就是界面，是用户能看见、能点、能操作的部分。 在哪里：网页、小程序、App 的界面。 用什么做：HTML（结构）、CSS（样式）、JavaScript（交互）。 02 后端（背后处理的部分） 是什么：后端就是幕后大脑，负责逻辑和运算。 在哪里：服务器上，用户看不见。 作用： 处理登录注册 计算价格、推荐商品 接收前端请求、返回数据 常用的编程语言：Java、Python、Go、JavaScript（Node.js） 03 数据库（记忆的部分） 是什么：数据库就是用来存储和管理数据的。 存什么：账号、密码、订单、库存… 常见的数据库： MySQL、PostgreSQL（关系型，像表格一样） MongoDB（文档型，像文件夹一样） 04 SaaS（软件即服务） 是什么：SaaS = Software as a Service（软件即服务）。 核心特点： 打开网页/小程序/APP就能用 按月或按年订阅 常见形式：网站、移动 App、小程序都可以是 SaaS。 05 总结 前端：用户看到和操作的界面 后端：背后运行的逻辑和计算 数据库：存放和管理数据的地方 SaaS：把软件做成服务，用户随时通过网络使用 前端展示 → 后端处理 → 数据库存储 → SaaS 是交付方式 帮我针对上面的内容画一张易于理解的图
```

Al centro, posiziona il kanji dominante sovradimensionato {argument name="main title text" default="全滅"} con un font massiccio e frantumato, rosso nella parte superiore che sfuma verso il giallo in quella inferiore, con texture di pietra incrinata, bordo nero, frammenti di detriti e bagliore d'impatto, che occupa gran parte dell'inquadratura. Nella parte inferiore centrale, aggiungi un banner nero inclinato con un bordo bianco contenente testo giapponese giallo brillante e rosso con la scritta {argument name="warning text" default="このままだと仕事がなくなる…!?"}, in grassetto e urgente. Sul lato destro, mostra un uomo giapponese fotorealistico ritagliato dal petto in su, rivolto leggermente a sinistra, che indossa una maglietta nera, con capelli scuri corti e una forte luce rossa sul bordo della silhouette; il suo volto è parzialmente oscurato da un blocco di censura a mosaico rettangolare nei toni del color carne. In basso a destra, includi esattamente 2 icone di app in stile Adobe, 「Pr」 e 「Ae」, quadrati arrotondati blu-viola scuro, parzialmente sovrapposti, entrambi incrinati come vetro rotto e barrati con un grande segno X diagonale di vernice rossa che attraversa le icone. Aggiungi una sottile silhouette dello skyline di una città scura nello sfondo in basso a sinistra. Aspetto generale: sensazionalistico, distruttivo, ad alto contrasto, grintoso, sovraccarico di texture, con un'intensa gradazione di colore rosso/nero, ombre spesse, bagliore drammatico e una composizione da miniatura virale rifinita.

Rendi tutto il testo giapponese e inglese nitido, leggibile e accuratamente composto, con l'atmosfera generale di una miniatura YouTube ad alta energia o di un banner per eventi sui social media per un workshop di design/AI.

La zona del suo volto è oscurata da un blocco rettangolare sfocato, mentre i suoi capelli selvaggi mossi dal vento sono nettamente sagomati contro un cielo arancione brillante con il sole basso e nuvole sparse. She raises a black wand overhead in her right hand, with a dangling orb charm at the tip glowing faintly purple and red. Her oversized crooked witch hat is black with purple lining and covered in stitched patches, warning labels, straps, and white graphics including a large “404” and a skull emblem. She wears a black and purple techwear outfit: oversized hooded jacket with many straps and tags, black crop top with “404” on the chest, layered belts, short bottoms, fishnet on one leg, black lace-up combat boots, chokers, and metallic accessories. Several hanging straps and tags visibly read words like “WITCH 404,” “404,” and glitch-themed markings. Emerging from the circle is 1 large armored summoned figure: a black futuristic demon-knight or robotic familiar with jagged reflective armor, a narrow purple-lit visor, and a heavy weapon held in one hand, partially dissolving into purple energy shards and smoke. The background shows a sprawling rainy megacity of apartment towers and industrial rooftops, packed with windows, balconies, cables, signs, and haze. On a nearby building wall is a giant vertical graffiti-style sign with 3 readable elements: “404”, “Witch”, and “ERROR NOT FOUND”, plus a smaller “E404”. Additional purple neon glitch text and symbols are scattered across rooftops and in the air. Use a dark palette of black, indigo, and deep violet with sharp magenta-purple highlights, cinematic contrast, reflective wet surfaces, dense detail, and a high-end polished illustration style. The mood is occult, edgy, stylish, and dangerous, combining urban fantasy, hacker aesthetics, and magical summoning.

```text
Un'inquadratura cinematografica ampia di un astronauta solitario in piedi su una sporgenza rocciosa, di spalle rispetto alla telecamera, che osserva un'immensa pianura desertica rossa su un pianeta simile a Marte. L'astronauta è centrato nella parte medio-bassa dell'inquadratura, indossa una realistica tuta spaziale EVA bianca con discreti pannelli grigi, uno zaino per il supporto vitale, stivali pesanti e guanti scuri; il casco non è visibile frontalmente poiché la figura è ripresa da dietro. Il paesaggio è vasto e arido, coperto di polvere color ruggine, pietre sparse e basse formazioni rocciose, con un aspro bordo di scogliera in primo piano a sinistra, cumuli rocciosi sparsi a media distanza e catene montuose nebbiose che si estendono lungo l'orizzonte lontano. Utilizzare tonalità calde color pesca e terracotta, una sottile foschia atmosferica e una luce tenue del tardo pomeriggio che proietta una lunga ombra dietro i piedi dell'astronauta. L'atmosfera è solitaria, carica di meraviglia ed esplorativa, come il fotogramma iniziale di un film di fantascienza sul primo contatto con un mondo alieno. Fotorealistico, alto dettaglio, composizione ultra-wide, color grading cinematografico naturale, scala epica, minimalismo, niente edifici, niente veicoli, niente testo, niente HUD, nessun'altra persona.
```

### 64. Elon Musk Douyin Livestream Screenshot

<img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/EvoLinkAI/awesome-gpt-image-2-prompts/main/images/ui_case36/output.jpg" alt="Elon Musk Douyin Livestream Screenshot" width="480">

- **Categorie:** UI & Social Media
- **Fonte:** [EvoLinkAI/awesome-gpt-image-2-prompts](https://x.com/Shinning1010/status/2046501587762188535)

**Prompt:**

```text
A 9:16 vertical version, high-detail realistic style Chinese TikTok live screenshot, Elon Musk is talking to the mobile phone camera in the live broadcast room, excited, smiling, and the live atmosphere is warm and real. He held a white handwritten sign in one hand, which clearly said: "Thank you Shinning". There are obvious Chinese TikTok interface elements in the live broadcast screen, including likes, comments and share icons arranged vertically on the right, scrolling Chinese bullet screens and interactive comments below, and the "live broadcast" logo at the top, which looks like a real mobile phone screenshot. There is an eye-catching gift prompt special effect in the screen: "Shinning sent TikTok No. 1", with gift animation light effect and platform-style prompt box. Musk is in a professional live broadcast environment, with a mobile phone holder, a ring fill light and a desktop microphone in front of him. The background is a modern technology live broadcast room with bright lights and a slight neon atmosphere. The composition is real and natural, like the ongoing live screenshot of the Chinese short video platform. The interface information is rich but not messy, the characters are clear, the expression is vivid, the details are rich, the sense of real photography, the depth of field, high definition, cinematic, photorealistic, realistic livestream screenshot, social media UI, Chinese Douyin live room, detailed lighting, natural skin texture.

Negative prompts:

Low definition, blur, cartoon, illustration, too strong CG sense, two-dimensional, deformed fingers, wrong text, scrambled code, multiple mobile phones, multiple brands, character repetition, face collapse, facial features distortion, excessive skin polishing, overexposure, too dark, messy background, wrong UI, non-Chinese short video interface, too many English bullet screens, gift special effects are not obvious, cropping error, proportional error

Supplementary reinforcement words:

Real mobile phone screen recording screenshot feeling, the live broadcast UI is complete, the gift prompt box conforms to the style of the Chinese short video platform, the Chinese comment area is active, the number of people online in the live broadcast room is clearly displayed, and the time, power and signal bar are visible.
```

```text
Frame 2 (Low Angle Power)

Frame 3 (Wide Isolation)

Frame 4 (Dynamic Close-Up Tilt)

Frame 5 (Motion Step Forward)
Mid shot, subject stepping forward dynamically, one leg extended, weight shifting, arms slightly swinging with controlled motion, shoulders rotated, head facing forward, gaze ahead, slight motion blur on edges, futuristic curved walls passing behind, soft studio lighting, shallow depth f/1.2, transitional energy frame, full beige suit, white t-shirt, white sleek sneakers, Peter Lindbergh influence
Frame 6 (Final Grounded Frame)
Full body frontal shot, subject standing firmly, legs slightly apart, arms held with controlled tension, shoulders squared, head facing forward, gaze into camera, symmetrical composition slightly offset, minimal futuristic background, soft lighting emphasizing structure, shallow depth f/1.2, strong final impact frame, subtle athletic readiness, full beige suit, white t-shirt, white sleek sneakers, Peter Lindbergh influence
```

<a id="prompt-3f484668ad2e4169c693"></a>

### 91. Crumple Chair Concept Sofa Development Board

<img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2/main/data/images/case370.jpg" alt="Crumple Chair Concept Sofa Development Board" width="480">

- **Categorie:** UI & Social Media
- **Fonte:** [freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://github.com/freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2#case-370)

**Prompt:**

```text
Design Concept: The Crumple Chair Core Philosophy: Translating the "controlled chaos" of a tossed paper ball into a sculptural, high-comfort seating experience.

Stage 1: Observation & Morphological Analysis The goal is to deconstruct the image of the crumpled paper into usable geometric data. Crease Mapping: Identify the primary "valley" and "ridge" lines. These represent potential structural ribs or seams in the chair. Faceted Planes: Break down the sphere into a series of non-uniform polygons. Each flat surface of the paper becomes a potential panel for the chair's upholstery or shell. Shadow Study: Analyze how the "tossed" form creates deep recesses. These natural pockets guide where the user's weight will be cradled.

Stage 2: Iterative Form Exploration Moving from a sphere to a seat through "Digital Crumpling." Subtractive Sculpting: Imagine the paper ball as a solid mass. Use Boolean operations to "carve out" a seating cavity that fits the human form while maintaining the external jagged texture. Tension Simulation: Use 3D software (like Rhino or Blender) to simulate a flat sheet of material being compressed. This ensures the folds look authentic and not "modeled." The "Toss" Logic: Experiment with gravity-based simulation dropping a digital mesh to see how it settles naturally, mimicking the "tossed" origin.

Stage 3: Ergonomic Translation & Blueprinting Refining the raw aesthetic into a functional object. The Comfort Core: Overlay a standard ergonomic template (Seating Angle: 105°–110°) over the crumpled form. Adjust the internal "folds" to provide lumbar support and pressure relief. Blueprint Generation: Create technical orthographic views (Front, Side, Top). Map out the dimensions: Seat Height: 450mm Total Width: 850mm Surface Smoothing: Maintain the sharp "paper edges" on the exterior shell while softening the interior contact points for skin comfort.

Stage 4: Structural Integration & Scaling Making the concept physically viable. The Skeleton: Design a hidden internal frame (likely CNC-bent steel rods or a 3D-printed lattice) that follows the most prominent ridges of the paper folds to provide rigidity. Material Selection: * Option A (High-End): Faceted, cast aluminum with a white powder coat. Option B (Soft): Vacuum-formed recycled plastic shell covered in "memory-fold" technical fabric that retains a wrinkled appearance.

Stage 5: Final Prototyping & Material Finish Textural Replication: Apply a matte, slightly porous finish to the material to mimic the tactile feel of heavy-bond paper. Lighting Contrast: Use directional studio lighting in the final renders to emphasize the "tossed" shadows, making the chair look like a giant piece of discarded inspiration. Design Tip: To keep the "tossed" look authentic, avoid symmetry. The most compelling aspect of a crumpled paper ball is its unique irregularity—ensure the left and right sides of the chair are balance-equivalent but not identical
```

They should be seated in an indoor leisure setting, such as a café or in front of a bar counter, with background props appropriate to the scene. Add a mobile system status bar UI at the very top of the image, including time, battery, signal, and network icons, making the entire image look like a phone screenshot. At the bottom of the image, overlay a wide semi-transparent galgame-style dialogue box. On the left side of the dialogue box, place an anime or chibi avatar corresponding to the character in the image. On the right side, format text with the character's name in a larger font on the first line, followed by one to two lines of gentle and healing-style simplified Chinese dialogue suitable for this character's personality, which you should create automatically. Below the dialogue box, add an operation bar imitating galgame UI. The overall style should be high-definition, rich in detail, with soft lighting, and naturally blending anime and real-life photography.

<img src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/88f33c6a-c307-4e7a-af7e-5f692cbf41a1" alt="Music Player Interface" width="480">

- **Categorie:** UI & Social Media
- **Fonte:** [ZeroLu/awesome-gpt-image](https://mp.weixin.qq.com/s/ASxig6mFVYxrIE8-8Fthew)

**Prompt:**

```text
Create a high-fidelity Chinese music app player interface screenshot in mobile portrait orientation, with refined visuals similar to a modern streaming player. Use dark mode, with the background derived from a blurred and diffused version of the album cover colors. Place a large square album cover in the center with subtle shadow and rounded corners.

Interface requirements:
- top status bar with time 18:26
- navigation bar with a back arrow on the left, a centered title, and a more-options icon on the right
- playback progress bar showing current time 01:42 and total duration 04:18
- playback controls for shuffle, previous, play/pause, next, and repeat
- lyrics section showing 5 to 7 scrolling lines, with the current line highlighted
- action row with like, comment, download, add to playlist, and share
- bottom area with device casting and playback queue entry

Use these exact Chinese labels and names:
- title: "正在播放"
- song name: "海边的晚风"
- artist: "林秋"
- album: "夏夜实验室"

The lyrics layout, button icons, reflections, shadows, and dark-mode layering should feel like a real production interface rather than a Dribbble concept.
```

### 120. Minimalist doodle sketch with white space

<img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2/main/data/images/case299.jpg" alt="Minimalist doodle sketch with white space" width="480">

- **Categorie:** UI & Social Media
- **Fonte:** [freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://github.com/freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2#case-299)

**Prompt:**

```text
Express [Subject/Theme] in a graffiti sketch style, presenting an overall visual effect of quick outlining, free deformation, impromptu hand-drawing, and draft-like appearance. The lines are casual, exaggerated, and can vary in thickness, slightly messy but rhythmic and expressive, emphasizing generalization, exaggeration, playfulness, and spontaneity, rather than rigorous realism or detailed rendering. Colors are expressed in rough blocks with a distinct dry-brush feel, retaining uneven smearing traces, brush strokes, dry-brush effects, and a sense of coverage. Colors automatically adapt to [Subject/Theme], but the overall expression remains graffiti-style, sketch-style, and generalized. Do not use transparent watercolor blooming effects, do not use delicate watercolor transitions, do not use paper textures, do not use soft atomization, and do not use dreamy textures. The background is mainly left blank, maintaining a sense of simplicity, relaxation, incompleteness, and design. A small number of auxiliary symbols, arrows, marks, circled areas, repeated lines, casually written text, or other graffiti elements can be added to enhance the visual language of a sketchbook or jotting style, but it must not be too crowded, and must not destroy the subject and the blank space temperament. The image content does not need to be written out in advance; the most suitable subject image, actions, related elements, symbols, or simplified scenes are automatically deduced and generated by [Subject/Theme], keeping the overall unified graffiti sketch style and exaggerated generalized expression, avoiding complex realistic backgrounds and over-elaboration. The exclusive signature "voxcat" needs to be naturally added to the image as a part of the picture. The position should be low-key but clear, and can be placed in the bottom left corner, bottom right corner, or near the title. The style must be consistent with the overall layout, like an artwork signature or a design sign-off; the signature font should be exquisite, restrained, and high-end, must not be too large, must not destroy the subject composition, and must not appear abrupt or cheap.
```

### 128. Fitness Training Infographic

<img src="https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HGbRt9DW8AA5V1S?format=jpg&amp;name=large" alt="Fitness Training Infographic" width="480">

- **Categorie:** UI & Social Media
- **Fonte:** [ZeroLu/awesome-gpt-image](https://x.com/MrLarus/status/2046627021674168640)

**Prompt:**

```text
Generate a Chinese fitness infographic for [topic].

The graphic should be both professional and practical for a normal adult to use as a training reference. Unless otherwise specified, assume the audience is a healthy adult with no major injuries, the goal is muscle gain plus basic strength improvement, the level is beginner to intermediate, the setting is a normal gym, and the total session length should stay within 40 to 60 minutes.

Choose the output format automatically based on the training topic:

1. If the topic is a muscle group or body part, such as chest, lats, biceps, abs, shoulders, or legs, generate a training-plan infographic for that body part.
2. If the topic is an exercise or skill goal, such as pull-up, push-up, dips, or squat, generate an exercise-unlock or progression-plan infographic.

Use a clear, modern, professional, easy-to-read Chinese infographic style in vertical format. The visual design should be clean and suitable for social sharing or personal training reference. Do not write long paragraphs. Each module should use short concise phrases, and numerical information should be prominent.

The infographic must include:
A. Title area with main title and subtitle
B. Training goal area
C. Warm-up area
D. Main training area with 4 to 6 core exercises
E. Progression or unlock logic area
F. Alternative exercises area
G. Execution reminders area
H. Recovery suggestions area
I. Visual design requirements: modern, clean, professional, modular card layout

The final result should be a complete infographic rather than plain text paragraphs.
```

### 132. A Chinese fitness infographic

<img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2/main/data/images/case183.jpg" alt="A Chinese fitness infographic" width="480">

- **Categorie:** UI & Social Media
- **Fonte:** [freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://github.com/freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2#case-183)

**Prompt:**

```text
Please generate a Chinese fitness infographic with the theme: 【xxx】.

The infographic should be both professional and practical, suitable for regular adults as a training reference. The default target audience is healthy adults without serious injuries; if no additional specifications are provided, the default training goal is "muscle building + basic strength improvement", the default training level is "beginner to intermediate", the default training scenario is "regular gym", and the default single training session duration should be controlled within 40-60 minutes.

Please automatically determine the output type based on the 【Training Theme】:

1) If the 【Training Theme】 is a specific muscle group or body part (e.g., chest, lats, biceps, abs, shoulders, legs, etc.), please output a "Training Plan Infographic for that Body Part".
2) If the 【Training Theme】 is a specific exercise or skill goal (e.g., pull-ups, push-ups, dips, squats, etc.), please output an "Exercise Unlocking/Progression Training Plan Infographic".

The entire infographic should adopt a clear, modern, professional, and readable Chinese infographic style, with vertical layout, clean visuals, and prominent highlights. It should be suitable for social media sharing or as a training reference card. Avoid lengthy paragraphs; present each section with concise short sentences, and make numerical information stand out.

This infographic must include the following content:

【A. Title Section】
- Main Title: Directly write 【Training Theme】 Training Plan / Unlocking Plan
- Subtitle: Automatically include target audience, goals, training scenario, and suggested duration
Example: Suitable for beginners / Muscle building focused / Gym version / 45 minutes

【B. Training Goals Section】
Explain in concise language:
- What this training primarily targets
- Main goals (muscle building / strength / skill unlocking / core control, etc.)
- Key stimulation or capability improvement direction for this training

【C. Warm-up Section】
Provide 2-4 warm-up suggestions, listed concisely, for example:
- Dynamic activities
- Target muscle group activation
- Light weight warm-up sets
Each item can include a brief description

【D. Main Training Section】
This is the core section, please list 4-6 main training exercises.
Each exercise must include the following information:
- Exercise name
- Training effect / Targeted area
- Sets × Reps (or duration)
- RIR recommendation
- Rest time between sets
- Key points of the exercise (1-2 points)
- Common mistakes (1 point only)

Ensure reasonable exercise arrangement:
- Compound exercises first, then isolation exercises
- Moderate overall training volume
- Don't schedule overly intense training for beginners
- Main exercises typically recommend RIR 1-3
- Isolation exercises can suggest RIR 0-2
- For abs or core exercises, use "seconds / reps" format
- For skill-based exercises, prioritize "prerequisite ability exercises + transition exercises + target exercise attempts"

【E. Progression/Unlocking Logic Section】
Automatically generate based on theme:
- For muscle group training: Write "How to progressively overload", e.g., increase weight after reaching rep limits, prioritize proper form, etc.
- For exercise unlocking: Write "Phased progression path", e.g., from hanging, scapular pull-ups, eccentric training, resistance band assistance, to completing the standard exercise

【F. Alternative Exercises Section】
Please provide 2-3 alternative exercises suitable for the following situations:
- No equipment available
- Home training
- Current ability is insufficient
- Certain exercises cannot be performed

【G. Execution Reminders Section】
Please provide 4-6 concise reminders, for example:
- Proper form takes priority over weight
- Don't train to failure on every set
- Allow 48-72 hours between training the same muscle group
- Pain doesn't equal normal exertion
- When sleep-deprived, appropriately reduce training volume

【H. Recovery Suggestions Section】
Briefly explain:
- Post-training recovery focus points
- Protein / sleep / recovery interval recommendations
- 1 risk reminder (if experiencing significant pain, stop and assess)

【I. Visual Design Requirements】
- Overall as a single-page Chinese infographic
- Vertical layout
- Modern, clean, professional, and strong fitness-oriented style
- Use modular card layout
- Key numbers (sets, reps, RIR, rest) should be prominent
- Can include simple human muscle group icons, dumbbells, barbells, pull-up icons, etc.
- Colors should be premium, clean, and sporty
- Chinese text must be clear, accurate, and readable
- Avoid excessive decorations, emphasize practicality and executability

Please finally output as "a complete infographic content", not just regular paragraph text.
```

### 146. Luxury Personal Color Profile Infographic

<img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2/main/data/images/case364.jpg" alt="Luxury Personal Color Profile Infographic" width="480">

- **Categorie:** UI & Social Media
- **Fonte:** [freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://github.com/freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2#case-364)

**Prompt:**

```text
LUXURY PERSONAL COLOR PROFILE — EDITORIAL LAYOUT
Studio portrait of subject as anchor — skin retouched to luminous glass-like perfection, preserved natural structure, realistic pore texture, soft directional key lighting, no facial alteration. Background: warm ecru parchment with subtle linen grain texture. Layout reads like a Vogue Italia beauty supplement printed on heavyweight matte stock. Structured editorial grid, 3-column asymmetric, wide negative space, serif condensed display headers, all labels in spaced uppercase tracking, cohesive warm ivory/sand/ecru background system throughout all panels, ultra-photorealistic 8K, soft diffused studio lighting, flat elegant surfaces, no drop shadows.
PANELS:
① UNDERTONE DIAGNOSIS — Tonal spectrum bar from cool ash to warm amber, precision needle marker on subject's reading. Labels: Cool / Neutral-Cool / Neutral / Neutral-Warm / Warm. Fine annotation text.
② SEASONAL COLOR PALETTE — 10–12 fabric-textured swatches in subject's optimal season. Each labeled with poetic color name and HEX. Grouped: Power Colors / Softest Options / Harmonizing Neutrals.
③ COLORS TO AVOID — Desaturated row of clashing tones with fine editorial strikethrough. Clean, non-harsh presentation.
④ MAKEUP CARTOGRAPHY — Eyeshadow gradient dust swatches / blush tones fanned on skin strip / lip spectrum barely-there to bold / highlighter finishes labeled: champagne, rose gold, pearlescent ivory.
⑤ HAIR COLOR SPECTRUM — Curved gradient strip: base, dimension, highlight, contrast tones. Gold bracket indicators on best options.
⑥ JEWELRY & METAL GUIDE — Flat-lay editorial render: yellow gold, rose gold, oxidized silver, platinum finishes alongside complementary stone tones. Minimal styling.
⑦ YOU IN YOUR PALETTE — 3–4 editorial lookbook frames, subject in palette-correct outfits. Mood labels: Quiet Luxury / Off-Duty Editorial / Evening Presence.
⑧ CAPSULE WARDROBE GRID — Outfit flatlay: tops, bottoms, outerwear, shoes, bag — all palette-correct. Coordinating lines showing interchangeability. Net-a-Porter editorial aesthetic.
⑨ PRINTS & PATTERNS — 4 fabric print thumbnails: micro geometric, tonal abstract, classic stripe, floral scale. One-line styling note per print.
⑩ STYLE ARCHETYPE — Single typographic panel. Style identity title set large (e.g. "Modern Romantic / Warm Classicist"). Three defining aesthetic words. Four-line editorial wardrobe philosophy note.
RENDER SPECS: Ultra-photorealistic, 8K, editorial magazine print quality, warm neutral color grading, soft diffused studio lighting consistent across all panels, one serif display font + one fine sans-serif body font, no gradients, flat matte surfaces only.
```

**Prompt:**

```text
[BRAND NAME = CHANGE TO YOUR BRAND].
Act as a Social Media Art Director and Digital Collage Artist specializing in bold, youth-oriented brand content for Instagram and digital campaigns. PHASE 1: CONCEPTUAL FRAMEWORK Create a dynamic digital collage that merges fashion photography with graphic design chaos. This is controlled rebellion – a composition that feels spontaneous and energetic while maintaining brand coherence. The aesthetic is anti-polished: torn edges, layered textures, hand-drawn elements, and bold color blocking that screams confidence and movement. PHASE 2: MODEL & PHOTOGRAPHY - Subject: One model (diverse casting, age 18-30) in a dynamic, confident pose - Pose Energy: 80% attitude, 20% natural – sitting, jumping, mid-motion, or power stance (avoid static standing) - Outfit: Street style/athleisure that aligns with [BRAND NAME] aesthetic – casual but styled - Hero Product: Feature 1 signature [BRAND NAME] product prominently (sneakers, bag, apparel) – this is the visual anchor - Photography Style: Editorial fashion cutout – model extracted from background with clean edges - Camera Angle: Slight low angle to empower subject (hero perspective) - Crop: Full body or 3/4 body showing hero product clearly - Background Removal: Model cut out cleanly for layering over collage elements PHASE 3: COLOR BLOCKING FOUNDATION - Primary Color Blob: Large organic shape (40-60% of composition) in bold, saturated brand color behind/around model - Shape Style: Irregular, hand-painted aesthetic – think Photoshop brush strokes or torn paper texture (NOT perfect geometric shapes) - Color Selection (Autonomous): Choose 1 hero color from [BRAND NAME] palette: - Texture: Visible brush strokes, grain, or subtle noise (15-25% opacity) – avoid flat digital fills - Placement: Blob positioned to frame model without obscuring key product details PHASE 4: GRAPHIC ELEMENTS LAYER Add 3-5 abstract graphic elements scattered across composition: - Element Types: - Color Palette: Use 2-3 accent colors total (main blob color + 1-2 contrasting tones from brand palette) - Placement: Asymmetric scatter – top-left and bottom-right zones primarily (avoid center crowding) - Scale: Mix small (5% of canvas) and medium (15% of canvas) elements – nothing overpowering - Aesthetic: Analog/handmade feel – imperfect circles, rough edges, visible texture PHASE 5: TYPOGRAPHY INTEGRATION - Brand Logo: Clean [BRAND NAME] logo placed in upper-left or upper-right quadrant (10-15% of width) - Slogan/Tagline: If [BRAND NAME] has an iconic slogan, integrate it using: - Supporting Copy: Optional 1-line descriptor (e.g., "A MOMENT OF YOUR STYLE") in smaller uppercase sans-serif - Type Treatment: Mix of aligned and slightly rotated text (2-5° angles) for dynamic energy - Hierarchy: Logo largest → Slogan medium → Copy smallest PHASE 6: TEXTURE & BACKGROUND - Base Layer: Off-white or light gray textured background (NOT pure white) - Texture Options (Autonomous selection): - Color: RGB 245-250 (near-white with warmth) – maintains brightness while adding depth - Treatment: Texture should be felt, not seen – enhances tactility without competing with foreground PHASE 7: COMPOSITION RULES - Layout: Asymmetric balance – model off-center, graphic elements counter-balance - Breathing Room: 15-20% negative space (textured background visible) to prevent claustrophobia - Layering Order: Background texture → Color blob → Graphic elements → Model (cutout) → Typography top layer - Focal Point: Model + hero product = primary focus (60% visual weight), graphics support (40%) - Movement: Diagonal lines and angled elements create directional flow (top-left to bottom-right or vice versa) PHASE 8: BRAND INTELLIGENCE (AUTONOMOUS) Autonomously adapt composition based on [BRAND NAME] personality: - Streetwear/Sportswear (Nike, Adidas, Supreme): - Luxury Streetwear (Balenciaga, Off-White, Gucci): - Beauty/Lifestyle (Glossier, Fenty, Skims): - Tech/Modern (Apple, Tesla, Beats): PHASE 9: SOCIAL MEDIA FOOTER (OPTIONAL) - Bottom Strip: Clean white or light gray bar at bottom 8-10% of frame - Content: Social media handles (Instagram, Facebook, Twitter) in small sans-serif - Layout: Three-column grid with platform icons or text handles - Aesthetic: Minimal and professional – contrast with chaotic collage above TECHNICAL SPECS: - Aspect Ratio: 4:5 (Instagram feed) or 1:1 (square social post) - Resolution: 2400x3000px minimum (high-quality for zoom and detail) - Color Mode: sRGB, vibrant saturation (Instagram-optimized) - File Aesthetic: Digital collage that mimics analog craft (Photoshop + hand-drawn hybrid) - Model Photography: 85mm lens, f/2.8, shallow depth of field on original shoot (before cutout) - Style Reference: Nike social campaigns, Spotify wrapped graphics, Gen Z Instagram aesthetics, Hypebeast x streetwear collabs - Mood: Confident, energetic, youthful, authentic chaos, anti-corporate polish
```

### 199. Music Player Interface

<img src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/88f33c6a-c307-4e7a-af7e-5f692cbf41a1" alt="Music Player Interface" width="480">

- **Categorie:** UI & Social Media
- **Fonte:** [ZeroLu/awesome-gpt-image](https://mp.weixin.qq.com/s/ASxig6mFVYxrIE8-8Fthew)

**Prompt:**

```text
Create a high-fidelity Chinese music app player interface screenshot in mobile portrait orientation, with refined visuals similar to a modern streaming player. Use dark mode, with the background derived from a blurred and diffused version of the album cover colors. Place a large square album cover in the center with subtle shadow and rounded corners.

Interface requirements:
- top status bar with time 18:26
- navigation bar with a back arrow on the left, a centered title, and a more-options icon on the right
- playback progress bar showing current time 01:42 and total duration 04:18
- playback controls for shuffle, previous, play/pause, next, and repeat
- lyrics section showing 5 to 7 scrolling lines, with the current line highlighted
- action row with like, comment, download, add to playlist, and share
- bottom area with device casting and playback queue entry

Use these exact Chinese labels and names:
- title: "正在播放"
- song name: "海边的晚风"
- artist: "林秋"
- album: "夏夜实验室"

The lyrics layout, button icons, reflections, shadows, and dark-mode layering should feel like a real production interface rather than a Dribbble concept.
```

<a id="prompt-c1550fbabdc1b75dd7dd"></a>

### 200. Fitness Training Infographic

<img src="https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HGbRt9DW8AA5V1S?format=jpg&amp;name=large" alt="Fitness Training Infographic" width="480">

- **Categorie:** UI & Social Media
- **Fonte:** [ZeroLu/awesome-gpt-image](https://x.com/MrLarus/status/2046627021674168640)

**Prompt:**

```text
Generate a Chinese fitness infographic for [topic].

The graphic should be both professional and practical for a normal adult to use as a training reference. Unless otherwise specified, assume the audience is a healthy adult with no major injuries, the goal is muscle gain plus basic strength improvement, the level is beginner to intermediate, the setting is a normal gym, and the total session length should stay within 40 to 60 minutes.

Choose the output format automatically based on the training topic:

1. If the topic is a muscle group or body part, such as chest, lats, biceps, abs, shoulders, or legs, generate a training-plan infographic for that body part.
2. If the topic is an exercise or skill goal, such as pull-up, push-up, dips, or squat, generate an exercise-unlock or progression-plan infographic.

Use a clear, modern, professional, easy-to-read Chinese infographic style in vertical format. The visual design should be clean and suitable for social sharing or personal training reference. Do not write long paragraphs. Each module should use short concise phrases, and numerical information should be prominent.

The infographic must include:
A. Title area with main title and subtitle
B. Training goal area
C. Warm-up area
D. Main training area with 4 to 6 core exercises
E. Progression or unlock logic area
F. Alternative exercises area
G. Execution reminders area
H. Recovery suggestions area
I. Visual design requirements: modern, clean, professional, modular card layout

The final result should be a complete infographic rather than plain text paragraphs.
```
